headline: Is the Market Bottoming? 3 Tells to Watch
<<<SUMMARY>>>
The 2026 market experienced a capitulatory reversal following midterm election results, with the Nasdaq recovering from a 2% futures decline. Analysts identify three key confirmations needed for a sustained bottom: Lowry Upside Days showing overwhelming buying demand, stabilization in leading AI stocks, and continued weakness in crude oil to ease rate pressures.

headline: The Wait Is Over: 2 Historic Artificial Intelligence (AI) Stock Splits Were Just Announced
<<<SUMMARY>>>
Meta Platforms and Sandisk announced their first-ever forward stock splits to make shares more accessible to retail investors. Meta is conducting an unorthodox 2-for-1 split through a new non-voting share class, while Sandisk approved an aggressive 8-for-1 split following a 6,000% rally since its February 2025 spinoff from Western Digital. Both companies are benefiting from AI demand, though Sandisk faces cyclical risks in the memory storage market.
<<<INSIGHTS>>>
META | positive | Company announced first-ever stock split to improve accessibility, integrating generative AI into ad platforms with improved pricing power, and recently launched Muse AI tool. Strong profitability foundation and multiple growth catalysts support positive outlook.
METAM | positive | Company announced first-ever stock split to improve accessibility, integrating generative AI into ad platforms with improved pricing power, and recently launched Muse AI tool. Strong profitability foundation and multiple growth catalysts support positive outlook.
SNDK | neutral | While the company shows exceptional growth (6,000% rally, 85% gross margin, EPS projected to jump from <$10 to ~$228), the article warns of significant cyclical risks in memory/storage markets and potential AI bubble concerns. Strong near-term demand offset by long-term uncertainty.
WDC | neutral | Mentioned as parent company of Sandisk spinoff in February 2025. No specific performance details or sentiment drivers provided in the article.
